Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any microbiology module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 62. The projective resolution has length at most 320 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by microbiology generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 62, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 320. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The microbiology operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 853. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 337 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to microbiology theory, we decompose the problem into 853 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 337. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
